## From the manufacturer

![Image 81: gearwrench, automotive, wrench, tools, ratchet, socket, professional, crescent, mechanic](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 83: Bolt Bite, Bolt Bitter, Bolt Biter, boltbiter, boltbitter, bolt grip, boltgrip, extraction](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 85: gearwrench, automotive, wrench, tools, ratchet, socket, professional, crescent, mechanic](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

### GEARWRENCH Bolt Biter

The dual sized GEARWRENCH Bolt Biter sockets allow users to work on both SAE and metric fasteners. The minus-sized sockets have been designed to fit on extremely worn-down fasteners without needing to pound the socket on with a hammer.

*   Bi-Directional Design for Removing or Tightening Damaged Fasteners
*   Tapered Entry Enables Socket to Easily Seat on a Damaged Fastener
*   Square Drive with Hex Base for Use with Impact Gun, Ratchet, Wrench, or Pliers
*   Chrome Molybdenum Alloy Steel (Cr-Mo) for Exceptional Strength and Long Lasting Durability
*   Black Oxide Coating Provides Superior Corrosion Resistance

### GEARWRENCH Bolt Biter Features

![Image 87: Bolt Bite, Bolt Bitter, Bolt Biter, boltbiter, boltbitter, bolt grip, boltgrip, extraction](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The innovative tapered flutes of the GEARWRENCH Bolt Biter sockets allow them to easily seat onto any fastener, including extremely damaged fasteners.

![Image 89: Bolt Bite, Bolt Bitter, Bolt Biter, boltbiter, boltbitter, bolt grip, boltgrip, extraction](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

GEARWRENCH Bolt Biter sockets can be used with both hand and power tools.

![Image 91: Bolt Bite, Bolt Bitter, Bolt Biter, boltbiter, boltbitter, bolt grip, boltgrip, extraction](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The GEARWRENCH Bolt Biter sockets allow you to reuse all of your fasteners.

![Image 93: gearwrench, automotive, wrench, tools, ratchet, socket, professional, crescent, mechanic](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

## Product Description

Bolt biter is the revolutionary new extraction socket system from GEARWRENCH. We talked with auto mechanics and industrial techs to learn what they hated about existing extraction sockets. Bolt biter redefines extraction and solves all of their frustrations.

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.
